# Index of every gate the code reads (generated)

Generated by `npm run gates:index` from the code; do not edit by hand. One line per `GATE_*` variable read in `server/` or `client/src/`: the file(s) that read it and, when `server/config/feature-gates.js` describes it in its header, that description. A gate with a paragraph in `docs/gates-and-env.md` has its full contract there; a gate with only a line here is documented by the comments at its reader.

Read at call time via adminMfaEnforceLive(); unset = enrollment stays optional.

- `GATE_ADS_GAP_RULE` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — the 2-hour Google Ads budget job decides from the technician's next 7 days of open stops instead of one day: runs the ad at its base budget while open stops are plentiful, drops it to the 1% floor when few are left, and keeps its state between; the owner can also hold a campaign at base or stop until a chosen end date. 'shadow' records what the new rule would do and changes nothing; 'true' makes it the rule. Read at call time via adsGapRuleMode(); unset or anything else = off, byte-identical to today. Changes ad budgets only; sends nothing to a customer

- `GATE_AGREEMENT_RENEWAL_REMINDER` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — the daily warranty renewal reminder also covers the Commercial Pest Service Agreement: each signed agreement gets renewal_date = its term anchor (the start date staff typed at issue, else the customer's first visit on or after signing, else, 180 days after signing with no visit, the signing date) + 12 months, rolled forward each year, and ONE office bell 45 days before the term ends ("Customers — send <name> the agreement renewal notice"), closed when staff mark the notice sent on Customer 360 or the agreement is cancelled. - `GATE_AIO_GAP_SWEEP_MONTHLY` — `server/config/feature-gates.js`
- `GATE_AI_CHANGE_CHECK` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — owner 2026-10-30, "all": before-and-after AI check for every content change. The daily impact tracker compares how often AI answers cited a changed page in the 14 days before its deploy with the 14 days after, adjusted for site-wide Waves citations, and stores gained / lost / flat / no_data on the content_optimization_impact row. A "lost" verdict adds one FIX leg to the impact digest (also needs GATE_IMPACT_DIGEST), at most one email a day. Reads seo_llm_mentions that the daily prober already stored; fires no probes, costs $0, sends nothing to a customer. Read at call time via aiChangeCheckLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = no check runs and the digest has no such leg.
- `GATE_AI_CONTENT_REPORT` — `server/index.js`, `server/routes/ai-assistant.js`
- `GATE_AI_CREDIT_KEYS` — `server/config/feature-gates.js`, `server/services/llm/credit-key-pool.js` — owner 2026-10-29: the owner's Claude Max / ChatGPT Pro plans each give a monthly API credit that lands in its own API organization. With this on and ANTHROPIC_CREDIT_KEYS / OPENAI_CREDIT_KEYS set (comma-separated, one credit-only key per organization), Anthropic and OpenAI calls that carry the main key are tried on the credit keys first, in order, and fall back to the main key when a credit key runs out of credit, is rejected or is rate limited (paused in memory 6 h, 1 h or the retry-after time). Only stateless calls move (messages, count_tokens, chat completions, embeddings, moderations, images, audio, and /v1/responses with store:false); file, container, batch, session and streamed-body calls stay on the main key. One wrapper around globalThis.fetch installed at boot by server/services/llm/credit-key-pool.js, so a flip needs a redeploy. Strict 'true', dark by default; off = nothing wrapped, every call as before. Sends nothing to a customer.
- `GATE_ALERT_CATALOG` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — owner 2026-10-29, "ok go" on admin bell rebuild step D1: every notification-registry type declares how it closes, and an admin alert the 08-28 bell policy silences is counted. `watch` = when NotificationService.create silences an admin row it writes ONE audit_log row (notification.bell_policy.muted) per Eastern day per distinct alert, category + trigger key + digit-free title stem only; the bell, pushes and create()'s return are unchanged. Anything else = off, byte-identical: no read, no write. Read at call time via alertCatalogMode(). See docs/gates-and-env.md.

- `GATE_AUTOPAY_HOLD_TIMEOUT` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — owner 2026-10-26, "OK FIX": an invoice reminder sequence held for Auto Pay (status autopay_hold) is released into the normal reminder ladder when Auto Pay never charged it. Only a charge failure used to release a hold, so an invoice Auto Pay never charges (for example an annual-prepay invoice staff created after a visit) got no reminder and no charge, forever. A hold older than 3 days with no stripe_invoice_charge_attempts row for the invoice at all, no payments row for the customer since the hold began and no failed payment still on the Auto Pay retry ladder is released through releaseFromAutopayHold(); terminal, payer-billed and payment-plan invoices, an invoice whose linked visit is still open, and a customer the customer-level reminder engine owns or covers are left alone. `shadow` = write one audit_log row per would-release invoice a day (invoice_followup.autopay_hold_timeout_shadow), change nothing; `true` = release; `charge` (owner 2026-10-28, "Charge the saved card"; narrowed 2026-10-29, option B) = charge the customer's saved Auto Pay card or bank for a held VISIT bill only, and only inside the visit-completion rail's own authorization (completion-charge-verdict.js: the visit's agreed price cap, the same lanes; an issued-invoice closeout counts as a performed visit for this charge only). Keep the hold when the charge went through or its outcome is unclear (an office alert says check Stripe). Over the agreed price, no agreed price, a visit that was not performed, a payer, or any guard refusal = the hold is kept, nothing is charged or texted, one office alert asks for a review (it is looked at again only after the bill or visit price changes). Release the hold only when the card was really declined or there is no Auto Pay method; an invoice bound to no visit, an annual-prepay term rail invoice, or a bill no completion lane admits is released as `true` releases it, never charged (one office alert, one audit_log row per attempt, invoice_followup.autopay_hold_timeout_charge). `charge` MOVES MONEY: the owner flips it. Anything else = off, no query. Read at call time via autopayHoldTimeoutMode(). Runs inside the invoice follow-up job. See docs/gates-and-env.md.

- `GATE_AUTO_DISPATCH_CATCH_UP` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — owner 2026-10-26 ("build A"): a 5:00 AM Eastern catch-up run of the auto-dispatch night tool. The 04:10 run is cut off when a deploy restarts the server mid-run (2026-09-03 and 2026-10-26: nothing moved, nothing re-ran it). On, a 05:00 job checks whether today already has a finished night run (cron or catch_up, completed or completed_with_errors); if not, it marks any cron run left 'running' by a dead process as failed and runs the night tool once more (triggered_by catch_up, at most one catch-up a day), under the same run lock. It uses the same mode and rules as the 04:10 run. Strict opt-in: exactly 'true', read at call time via autoDispatchCatchUpLive(). Ships DARK; off = the 05:00 job does nothing and reads nothing. Sends nothing to a customer.

- `GATE_AWAY_WATCH` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — WaveGuard perk Away Watch + Welcome Home, owner approved 2026-10-31 and told us to build 2026-11-01: a WaveGuard member (isMembershipCustomerRow; every surface and every consumer checks it, so dates on a non-member's row do nothing) sets the day they leave and the day they are back, in the customer app (Property tab) or in Customer 360 (a technician's 360 view never carries the dates). On each visit inside that window the tech job card and the tech Visit Brief (GET /admin/schedule/:id/visit-brief, key awayWatch, after the reassignment recheck) say to take 3 photos of the outside of the home from the ground and note standing water, the closeout refuses a performed visit with fewer than 3 photos (away_watch_closeout_photos_upload_required; the body's completion photos, the visit photos staged from Tech Home and a linked lawn assessment's customer-visible photos each count once), and the last visit before the return day also says to do a lanai cobweb sweep (no product). The photos reach the member in the normal completion text and service report; it sends no new message. Strict opt-in: exactly 'true', read at call time by server/config/feature-gates.js awayWatchLive(). Dark by default; off = the two writers ignore away_watch_from / away_watch_until, neither GET returns them, the job card and closeout are unchanged. Not a security or home-watch service. The pest Away Mode (property_preferences.away_mode_until) is a separate feature.

Admin bell only: nothing goes to a buyer, contractor or agency. - `GATE_BID_DESK` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — Bid Desk, PR 1: every 15 minutes a job reads new rows of the emails table and files bid invites (BuildingConnected, Procore, bid portals, government RFP/RFQ mail) as bid opportunities with their due dates read from the fixed provider templates only, posts ONE admin notification per new bid, one when an addendum arrives on an open bid, and reminders 7 and 2 days before a due date the operator CONFIRMED; the admin Bid Desk page lists them and lets the operator confirm the date and set bid / no bid / submitted / won / lost. No AI call, no change to leads or the classifier, and it sends nothing to a buyer, contractor or customer. Read at call time via bidDeskLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = no job work, the routes answer 404 and the nav entry is hidden.
- `GATE_BID_DESK_AUTO_DATES` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — Bid Desk, needs GATE_BID_DESK: a due date read from a provider's fixed email template, or a SAM.gov response deadline, counts as confirmed when the bid is first filed, so the 7-day and 2-day reminders start by themselves; and an open bid still marked new, with no submission, closes itself as no bid once its due date is more than a day past (a date still waiting on an addendum check waits 30 days), with a note saying so. A date from a later email, an addendum or a SAM.gov deadline move still waits for the operator. Admin portal only: it sends nothing to a buyer, contractor, agency or customer and rings no new bell. Read at call time via bidDeskAutoDatesLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = the Bid Desk works as before.
- `GATE_BID_DESK_CHARLOTTE_DATES` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — Bid Desk, needs GATE_BID_DESK: an open Charlotte County Purchasing bid with a bid number and no due date gets its date from the county's own public bid list (purchasingbids.charlottecountyfl.gov, one read, at most hourly, and only while such a bid is waiting); the date counts as confirmed only while GATE_BID_DESK_AUTO_DATES is also on. An existing date, a decided bid and every other source are never touched. Admin portal only: it sends nothing to a buyer, contractor, agency or customer and rings no new bell. Read at call time via bidDeskCharlotteDatesLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = no call to the county and the Bid Desk works as before.
- `GATE_BID_DESK_NOT_OUR_WORK` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — Bid Desk, needs GATE_BID_DESK: a NEW bid whose title names grounds upkeep (mowing, edging, trimming, grounds or lot maintenance, tree work and the like) and names none of our work (herbicide, fertilizer, pest, weed control and the like) is filed as no bid with a note, and rings no new-bid bell. A bid a person marks by hand is never declined. Existing bids are never touched. Admin portal only: it sends nothing to a buyer, contractor, agency or customer. Read at call time via bidDeskNotOurWorkLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = every bid is filed as before.
- `GATE_BID_DESK_SAM` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — Bid Desk, PR 2: once a day at 7:25 AM ET a job reads new federal notices from the official SAM.gov Opportunities API (NAICS 561710 pest control and 561730 landscaping, Florida, solicitations, combined synopses, presolicitations and sources sought) whose place of performance is in the Tampa Bay to Southwest Florida ZIP areas, and files each as a bid opportunity (source sam_gov, one per notice id) with its SAM.gov link and response deadline (never confirmed until the operator confirms it), posting ONE admin notification per new bid. Needs GATE_BID_DESK and SAM_GOV_API_KEY. No AI call; it sends nothing to an agency, contractor or customer. Read at call time via bidDeskSamLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = the job returns before any request.
- `GATE_BID_KIT` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — Bid kit, admin only: one page for the documents and company facts every government bid asks for (business license copy, certified operator certificate and notarized full-time statement, employee ID cards, product list, insurance certificate, W-9, Sunbiz status, tax receipt, E-Verify, references and the company fact sheet), each with its file, limits, source document, checked-on date, expiry and the owner's sign-off, plus a facts sheet the owner copies from while filling agency forms by hand. Files sit in private storage and open only by a short signed link. Never public, never on the website, never sent to anyone, no AI. Once a day at 6:05 AM ET (the compliance slot) a job posts one admin notification per item that expired or expires within 30 days (once a week per item). Read at call time via bidKitLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = every /api/admin/bid-kit route answers 404, the nav entry is hidden and the daily job does nothing.
- `GATE_BID_KIT_PRODUCT_LIST_SYNC` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — owner 2026-10-25: once a day in the 6:05 AM ET compliance slot the Bid kit Product List PDF (every product applied on a visit in the last 12 months, with active ingredient, EPA Reg. No., Label and SDS links) is rebuilt from the visit records. A changed list replaces the file; when ONLY products bought from SiteOne changed and the list is complete (every pesticide has an EPA number, label and SDS) the kit signs it off itself and logs an automatic sign-off, otherwise the file is left NOT signed off and ONE admin bell says what changed. Needs GATE_BID_KIT too. Read at call time via bidKitProductListSyncLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = the job returns before any query. Sends nothing to anyone.

The live owner answer already reads such an invoice as the payer's. Sends nothing. An unknown value is null with a reason, never 0. The agent prompt and the tool description gain the scorecard rules. Read at call time via biTechScorecardLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = the tool output and the prompt are byte for byte today's. Dark = the generic unknown-route 404 for EVERY request to the path, before the route's own rate limiter, per the house dark-GATE_* contract. - `GATE_BLOG_SEARCH_SUGGEST` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — Suggest a post from the completion forms' blog search, owner mockup approval 2026-10-03; owner ruling 2026-10-02: straight into the autonomous blog queue, written and published automatically, no approval step. With GATE_REPORT_BLOG_POST on, a search no live post holds every word of says so, shows the closest posts and offers "Suggest a post about …" to the office (an admin login only): POST /admin/dispatch/:id/blog-suggestions writes one operator-pinned new_supporting_blog row into opportunity_queue (bucket operator_intercept, score 79, no city, service and specialty topic inferred from the phrase, signal_metadata.source tech_blog_search; unlike a manifest intercept it is SERP-profiled, the router's public-health, navigational and do_not_publish demotions apply to it, and the quality gate waives only GSC evidence), refusing a near-me, out-of-area or personal-data phrase (a customer's name in our records included), one with a letter outside a-z, a search a live post now covers, a topic the chain tried and skipped (declined), or more than 10 a person a day; the same phrase from any source answers already_queued. The tip seeder (server/scripts/seed-tech-tip-blogs.js, tips with facts) writes through the same path, at most five hand-picked tip topics a run, and refuses to write while this gate is off. The phrase is never logged. Strict opt-in: exactly 'true', read at call time via blogSearchSuggestLive(). Ships DARK; off = the search answers suggest:false, the forms show no button, the route answers 404 { enabled: false }, and suggestions already queued are not claimed (opportunity-queue fences source tech_blog_search; they wait, and expire unclaimed after 45 days).

- `GATE_BOOK_ARRIVAL_GRACE` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — owner-approved 2026-09-29: the SAME self-serve arrival grace the estimate picker already uses (SELF_SERVE_ARRIVAL_GRACE_MINUTES, above) now also applies to ONLINE BOOKING — /book offers (/api/booking/availability, /find-slots, the /capture-intent revalidation, public re-service, inspection booking) and their commit, createSelfBooking. **Ships DARK: off unless `true`/`1`/`on`**; needs GATE_BOOK_CAPACITY_COMMIT + GATE_SCHEDULING_CAPACITY live too (bookInsertionOffersLive()) and a grace value above 0 to change anything; read at call time via bookArrivalGraceLive() (config/feature-gates.js). Off = today's strict drive+15-minute travel-gap offer/commit, byte for byte. On: (1) the offer keeps a slot the strict gap would drop when the day's whole-route arrival simulation certifies the technician arrives within grace of the slot's start — waived ONLY against the PREVIOUS assigned committed stop (never a real window overlap, never a live hold, never the NEXT stop's side, never an unassigned/other-technician stop) and only when every other neighbour clears the strict gap; (2) find-time.js's packCapacityEnds now checks BOTH route neighbours of a candidate under that same rule (it used to test the previous-side pick against the previous stop only and the next-side pick against the next stop only, so picks that crowded the OTHER neighbour survived find-time and were dropped by /book's own mirror, emptying whole days); (3) the commit's strict pre-verify travel probe accepts exactly those waived previous-side clashes and verifyArrivalCapacity enforces the same grace bound, so offer and commit agree in both directions; (4) the offer's signed slot_sig carries a distinct policy tag (BOOK_ARRIVAL_GRACE_OFFER_POLICY, utils/slot-offer-token.js) and the EXACT grace that justified it (v3 field shape `<exp>.<grace>.<sig>`, only when grace > 0), so a flip between mint and confirm — either direction — fails the signature into the standard pick-your-time-again 409. The phone agent, public reschedule (its rebooker commit still runs the strict probe), office Find-a-Time, Intelligence Bar and auto-dispatch never opt in and are unaffected. Kill switch: unset it.

Only the Waves Assessment catalog row (every service view of the call must resolve to it) and only when no price came up at all; any price mentioned goes to the priced path (GATE_CALL_COMMERCIAL_DICTATED_BOOKING), unchanged. The staff commitment and the caller acceptance are grounded word for word in the right speaker's turns (call-reschedule-agreement.js), including the shape where STAFF proposes the day and time, the caller says yes and staff then commits ("How does noon on Thursday sound?" / "Perfect." / "I'll book you for that"), which has its own extraction instruction while this gate is on. On an OUTBOUND recording the speaker labels are LLM-inferred and have swapped, so staff identity is proven independent of the labels: an Agent-labeled turn says "this is <name> with|from|at Waves" and no Caller-labeled turn does; otherwise it holds. Also needs GATE_CALL_AGENT_COMMIT_BOOKING (its kill switch) and GATE_CALL_AGENT_COMMIT_TRUSTED_LABELS. OUTBOUND calls qualify only as lead callback bridges (call metadata.type lead_auto_bridge) AND only when outbound booking creation itself is on: GATE_CALL_OUTBOUND_BOOKING plus V2 routing in enforce mode (CALL_EXTRACTION_V2_ENABLED=true and CALL_EXTRACTION_V2_DRIVES_ROUTING=true or CALL_TRIAGE_ENFORCE_V2_GATES=true); this gate never authorizes outbound creation by itself, and the routing lane and the creation path read one predicate (outboundAutoBookingEnabled). Only clears commercial_requires_quote and the advisory card still files; the on-the-hour rule, capacity, address validation and unit checks still apply. Strict opt-in, read at call time via callCommercialAssessmentBookingLive(). Off = byte-identical, outbound stays excluded. See services/call-commercial-dictated-booking.js.

The staff commitment quote AND the caller's acceptance quote must each appear word for word in a turn of their own speaker (call-reschedule-agreement.js groundRescheduleAgreement, reused unchanged); a missing/unlabeled transcript or a quote only in the other speaker's turn fails closed. Also needs GATE_CALL_AGENT_COMMIT_BOOKING (its kill switch) and GATE_CALL_AGENT_COMMIT_TRUSTED_LABELS. Only clears commercial_requires_quote — capacity, address validation, unit checks and the on-the-hour rule still apply, and no price agreed still goes to the office. - `GATE_CALL_EXTRACTION_SYSTEM_PROMPT` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — call extraction V2 sends its rules and output contract as a static system part and only the call's own facts and transcript as the user message, so the provider can cache the rules across calls. Same rules, different order and channel: its calls are their own prompt-version cohort (`s`, or `b` with the agent-proposed-slot block). Strict opt-in, read once per call via callExtractionSystemPromptLive(). Off = the one-message prompt, byte-identical. Rollback: unset.
- `GATE_CALL_EXTRACTION_V1_FALLBACK` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — call extraction V1 (extractCallData, the legacy extractor that still feeds the routing legs) runs on Gemini alone. When Gemini fails for a PROVIDER reason (a non-2xx answer such as 402 credits depleted, 429 or 5xx; a network error or timeout; GEMINI_API_KEY missing; or invalid JSON after the existing two parse attempts), this gate retries the SAME prompt through the house dispatcher: Claude first (MODELS.CALL_EXTRACTION_ANTHROPIC, pinned like the V2 Claude leg), then OpenAI (the pinned CALL_EXTRACTION_MODEL leg) if Claude also fails. Output goes through the same normalizeCallExtraction tail, so its shape is identical; the provider that served it is stamped as extraction_v1_provider in metadata.processing_timings and a warn line names the fallback. A bad answer that is not a provider failure keeps today's behavior, and when every backup leg fails the call takes the same path as before (extraction_failed with the attempt counter, or the invalid-JSON stub). Strict opt-in, read at call time via callExtractionV1FallbackLive(). Off = Gemini only, byte-identical. Rollback: unset. Sends nothing to a customer.

- `GATE_CALL_PROMISE_AI_CLOSE` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — owner 2026-10-24: an open Waves call promise closes on its own when the same AI check the text-follow-up lane uses finds, across every line (texts both ways, calls, emails, visits booked, moved or completed, estimates, payments), a later record that kept it, quoted exactly; a customer text confirming it was done also closes it. Read at call time via callPromiseAiCloseLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = exactly today's behavior. Needs GATE_CALL_COMMITMENTS and PROMISE_EVIDENCE_CLOSE. Staff can Reopen from the Owed tab. Sends nothing to a customer.
- `GATE_CALL_PROMISE_LATER_TEXT_CHECK` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — owner 2026-10-25 "build it": a record proof the call-promise AI check accepted is no longer held for good by any later human text. The AI reads the texts that came after the proof and answers one question: does any of them say this promise was not kept, was undone, went wrong or is still needed? Only a clear no lets the proof close the promise; yes, unclear, a failed read, any later message with no words (a photo) or too long to read whole, more later texts than it reads, and card-number fragments it cannot attribute keep it held as before. Needs GATE_CALL_PROMISE_AI_CLOSE. Read at call time via callPromiseLaterTextCheckLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = exactly today's behavior. Staff can Reopen from the Owed tab. Sends nothing to a customer.

Each match costs one extra transcription pass; the log line "[call-proc] spelling collapse trigger" names which signal fired. Strict opt-in, read at call time via callSpellingCollapseTriggerLive(). Off = the pass runs only on the signals it ran on before, byte-identical. Rollback: unset. See services/contact-dictation.js spellingCollapseSignals.

Records who sold an estimate; pays, texts and charges nothing, the commission ledger is part 3b-2. On: the field-lead flag also records a GPS check (customer flag: a truck arrival at the visit in the 12 hours before; new 'Neighbor' flag: the truck within field_flag.gps_radius_m, default 300 m, of the typed address, position under 6 hours old; distance only, never the truck's coordinates), a verified flag from an active W-2 technician fills estimates.sold_by_technician_id while the estimate is not accepted and not cleared, and admins can set or clear it (PUT /api/admin/estimates/:id/sold-by, locked once accepted). Read at call time via commissionSoldByLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = the flag route, the flag modal and every estimate path are byte for byte today's and nothing is read, written or shown; rollback = unset (stored Sold by values stay but nothing reads or shows them).

- `GATE_CONTACT_LOGIN` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — an on-location contact signs into the customer app with their OWN phone number through the same SMS code login, landing on the account they are a contact for as a CONTACT session: view-only schedule, visit history, technician tracking, service-report documents (never for a contact the report rule withholds from) and their own notification choices (GET/PUT /api/notifications/contact-preferences); every other portal route answers 403 ACCOUNT_HOLDER_ONLY — billing, payments, cards, referrals, credits, requests, chat, property, account deletion, the holder's preferences, push subscriptions. The account holder's own number always wins; a contact number is tried only when no holder row matches, and the contact's session dies at its next refresh once the holder removes them. Per-contact notification choices (service_contact_notification_prefs) only NARROW what the account sends: a purpose the holder turned off stays off. Owner rulings 2026-10-20: view only, no confirm or reschedule; the contact's in-app text-consent checkbox counts as the YES for a pending opt-in ask only, never over a STOP. Read at call time via contactLoginLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = a contact number never logs in, an existing contact token is refused (401) and the senders ignore the per-contact table, byte-identical to before. Sends nothing to a customer.
- `GATE_CONTACT_REPORT_TEXT` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — when the account holder's visit-complete text goes out, each confirmed on-location contact gets one plain text with the report link: no pay link, no review ask; the combined-stop summary text then goes to the account holder, not Contact 1; owner ruling 2026-10-03. Read at call time via contactReportTextLive(), dark by default; off = no contact text is queued, a queued one is dropped at its recheck, and the summary recipient is unchanged. The gate is the only supported switch: the contact_report_ready sms template row must stay active while it is on.

- `GATE_DICTATION_MONEY_NORMALIZE` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — owner 2026-10-21, after "sixty-one thirty-three" came back as 6133, $60.33 and $61.33: the staff mic's transcript is read for dollar amounts. In a money context only (a $ or a word like price, charge, rate, a month), the server SUGGESTS (never rewrites; the transcript is returned exactly as heard) "$61.33" for "sixty-one thirty-three", "61 33" or "sixty one dollars and thirty three cents" and "$105" for "a hundred and five"; a run-together "$6133" is kept as typed and flagged ambiguous, so the Intelligence Bar asks with answer buttons between $61.33 and $6,133. Phone numbers, street numbers, times, dates, ZIPs, invoice numbers and quantities with units are never touched. Strict opt-in: exactly 'true' in every environment, read at call time via dictationMoneyNormalizeLive(). Ships DARK; off = the transcript is returned as before. Sends nothing to a customer.

Admin only: the estimates page gets a "Call today" list (viewed offers 2 h to 48 h after the last open with no call or follow-up since; the list rows carry lastCalledAt from the newest outbound call to the estimate phone in the last 30 days that the customer answered, by the missed-call bell's answered-leg proof) and a "Pick reason" list (expired offers the sweep marked expired_viewed in the last 30 days), and PATCH /api/admin/estimates/:id accepts a staff loss reason on an expired row with no status change. Extending an expired offer clears a staff reason. Read at call time via estimateCallListLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; the admin client reads it as the `estimate_call_list` flag on GET /api/admin/feature-flags. Each returned note passes the structural link redaction (signed and private links) and ONE fixed-format secret scrubber (API key and token shapes, Bearer tokens, JWTs, PEM blocks, long base64/hex runs, credential query parameters in URLs, card numbers, SSN pattern), is cut at 1,000 characters, and the read keeps its untrusted-data marker so later writes show confirm cards. Labelled credentials (a credential key followed by ':' or '=') lose everything after the separator to the end of the note string. No other prose rules: a code or password written as plain words comes back as written. Read at call time via ibReadNotesLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = notes are withheld exactly as before.
- `GATE_IB_RECORD_PAYMENT` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — the Intelligence Bar's record_payment write tool, owner ruling 2026-10-12 "yes to all recommendations" on the Stage 3 plan: an admin can record money ALREADY RECEIVED outside the portal (cash, check, Zelle, Venmo, PayPal or other) against one open invoice on a confirm card (never owner-direct), by running the Invoices page's own Record payment handler (POST /admin/invoices/:id/record-payment) in process. Settles the whole amount due and sends NO receipt (a receipt is the separate resend_receipt tool or the Invoices page). B1: only 8:00 AM through 7:45 PM Eastern is accepted (the sender runs every five minutes with no quiet-hours check, so 7:45 PM is the latest time that always leaves before 8 PM even if two runs are missed; 8 PM to 8 AM is refused) and the bar asks for a time in that range; the page keeps its behavior. - `GATE_LEAD_EMAIL_LINKS` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — Activity timeline only: also lists email that was sent to a prospect before they became a customer, matched by the lead / estimate the send recorded (email_messages.lead_id / estimate_id) through leads.customer_id and estimates.customer_id, not by address. Strict opt-in, read at call time via leadEmailLinksLive(). Dark = the timeline lists exactly what it did before. Recording the link on each send is not gated (additive columns).
- `GATE_LEAD_ESTIMATE_AUTOMATION` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — generate priced lead draft estimates
- `GATE_LEAD_ESTIMATE_AUTO_SEND` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — auto-send generated lead estimates

- `GATE_LEAD_REPEAT_SWEEP` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — owner 2026-11-01, Action inbox agents A, "yes do this": a nightly 4:52 AM ET sweep files an unanswered lead that repeats the same person's open lead as its duplicate, so the inbox counts each person once and the person's real lead can settle. A waiting lead (the inbox's own leads_awaiting_contact queue) is filed only when an earlier live lead with the same phone, from the 30 days before it, leads to exactly one open root that has the same phone, a matching first or last name and no conflicting name, the same customer row when both are linked, the same street, unit and ZIP when both carry one, the same requested service by the quote wizard's own rule (catalog service_key, else the service label; no extra properties on either), and a live courtship (no closed estimate, shared with the wizard via lead-courtship.js). A won, lost or other closed earlier lead, another service, or a closed estimate never makes a repeat. The filing is the Leads page's own: status duplicate, the extracted_data.duplicate_of_lead_id marker the wizard's repeat dedupe writes, a status_change history row, then the funnel bridge. No first response is stamped. Sends nothing to anyone. Read at call time via leadRepeatSweepLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = no lead is touched. Undo a filing on the Leads page; the sweep never files that lead again. Kill switch: unset.
- `GATE_LEAD_SERVICE_DISPLAY` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — Lead service names, owner 2026-10-09: the lead list and lead detail responses carry `service_display`, the lead's service named the way the booking catalog names it, and the Leads screen shows it with the stored text as a tooltip; lead search also matches the shown name. Display only: `leads.service_interest` is never rewritten and nothing is sent to a customer. Strict opt-in: exactly 'true', read at call time via leadServiceDisplayLive(). Ships DARK; off = the screen shows the stored text.

- `GATE_LOOKUP_ADDRESS_STATUS` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — address-match PR 4: the admin estimate tool's property lookup shows one address status line from Google Address Validation, apart from the county roll's own answer: Address confirmed / Building confirmed, unit missing / Address needs confirmation / Outside the service area / Validation unavailable, with Google's business or residential classification. No scope effect: no measurement, flag, cache row or price changes. One validation call per address per 24 h, 4 s cap, fail-open to unavailable; needs ADDRESS_VALIDATION_ENABLED. Off unless exactly 'true', read at call time via lookupAddressStatusLive(); off = no call and a byte-identical response. Kill switch: unset.
- `GATE_LOOKUP_BUSINESS_IDENTITY` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — address-match PR 5: the admin estimate lookup and the estimator engine ask Google Places which operating business sits at the street number and read its type and tenant count, so a storefront with its own street number inside a plaza parcel is no longer priced off the whole building or a satellite guess. Needs GATE_COMMERCIAL_SUITE_SIZING and only runs for the opted-in admin lookup and engine, never a public route. A matched business with part-building evidence is one suite; one with none of that is scope_unresolved, no price until the CSR answers "just your space or the whole building?". Deterministic, no AI reaches a size or price. Off unless exactly 'true', read at call time via lookupBusinessIdentityLive(); off = no Places call and byte-identical output. Kill switch: unset.
- `GATE_LOOKUP_LISTING_SIZE` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — address-match PR 5b, owner 2026-10-02: a commercial suite with no caller-stated size is priced off a size a public listing publishes for THAT suite. Plain code reads Google result snippets (DataForSEO; LoopNet and Crexi refuse every server fetch) and fetchable broker pages for a figure next to the typed street number and suite; a range or two different figures never count; no model reaches the number. Ranks below a caller/tech-stated size, above the DBPR license seats and the type default; draft stays yellow with the link. Off unless exactly 'true', read at call time via lookupListingSizeLive(); off = no search, no fetch, byte-identical ladder. Kill switch: unset.
- `GATE_LOOKUP_PERMIT_FACTS` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — address-match round 2 / R2-B: the property lookup reads the home's own Manatee building permit facts (conditioned and under-roof square footage, stories; collected by GATE_PERMIT_DETAIL_SYNC into construction_permit_records) when the county roll has no building for the address, and the admin estimate tool and the estimator engine prefill home sq ft from them as a sourced estimate above the plat median, always flagged for confirmation and never as a measurement. Off unless exactly 'true', read at call time via lookupPermitFactsLive(); off = no permit read, byte-identical lookup, profile and draft. Kill switch: unset.
- `GATE_MANUAL_ACCEPT_CONSUMER_NOTICE_RECONCILE` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — daily catch-up for the Mark accepted consumer notice send, needs GATE_MANUAL_ACCEPT_CONSUMER_NOTICE_SEND: the send runs detached after the accept commits, so a restart in between loses it with no bell. The 6:10 AM ET document run re-runs the same send for an office accept from the last 21 days (none before the send went live on 2026-10-26) when no email or text of a 13692 Document Request went out since the accept and the office got no bell for it. Read at call time via manualAcceptConsumerNoticeReconcileLive(), strict 1/true/on, dark by default; off = no catch-up.
- `GATE_MANUAL_ACCEPT_CONSUMER_NOTICE_SEND` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — owner 2026-10-26, Rule 5E-14.105(1), F.A.C.: after the office's Mark accepted (or the Intelligence Bar accept) commits on an estimate with a termite line, and the customer has no signed FDACS-13692 on file, the consumer_notice.fdacs_13692 Document Request is issued (or the open one reused) and its signing link is sent by email and text through the Document Requests delivery; a failure rings the office bell. Read at call time via manualAcceptConsumerNoticeSendLive(), strict 1/true/on, dark by default; off = Mark accepted sends nothing new.

- `GATE_MOVE_NOTICE_EMAIL` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — owner 2026-10-24 "reschedule email": the email version of a reschedule or cancellation text that reached nobody. A customer whose line cannot take texts (a landline, a carrier bounce) otherwise hears nothing when a visit moves or is cancelled. On, when the reschedule text (handleReschedule, sendRescheduleNoticeForVisit) or the cancellation text (handleCancellation) reached no recipient and was not held (send window, visit move, unreadable settings), or a carrier later bounced it (handleUndeliveredSms), the customer gets the same notice by email (appointment.rescheduled / appointment.cancelled, transactional). No usable email raises the no-channel admin alert. One email per notice. Off = today's behavior: no reschedule or cancellation email. Kill: unset

Destinations are pre-registered outbound_links rows keyed by a hash of the URL — the route never reads a URL from the request, so it is not an open redirect — and are never tagged or altered. - `GATE_PAYROLL` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — basic payroll for Waves' own W-2 technicians, owner 2026-10-25, "can we start building basic now, and add layers to it at later stages?". Layer 1b-2a: an admin types the elections from a signed paper Form W-4 into the Staff page Payroll tab (filing status, Step 2 box, Step 3 credits, Step 4 amounts, exempt, date signed, first pay date it applies to); each entry is a new row and old rows are never edited. The admin also records how each technician is paid: paper check, or bank transfer with the date of the employee's written OK (Florida law). Stores no SSN and no bank account number; the paper W-4 keeps both. Read at call time via payrollLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = every /api/admin/payroll route answers 404 before any query and the Payroll tab is hidden; rollback = unset. Sends nothing to a customer or staff member.
- `GATE_PAYROLL_BANK_MATCH` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — basic payroll item 8, owner 2026-10-28, "pay package is still being built, do these": the bank import ties a bank line to the paycheck or payroll tax payment it paid. A debit on a business account equal to a finalized, not-voided paycheck's net pay (pay date minus 3 days to plus 45 days, or plus 7 for a bank transfer) links when exactly one check and one bank line pair up, and a check number printed on the line breaks a tie; an IRS / EFTPS debit equal to a payroll federal deposit calendar row, and a Florida Department of Revenue debit equal to a payroll RT-6 row, link the same way. A linked line is booked as an expense in "Payroll (counted from pay runs)", which the P&L and Schedule C draft leave out. Anything else (two checks with one amount, an amount one cent off, a different check number) stays a suggestion with the paycheck named, and the owner picks. An automatic link is verified right after it commits (a later same-amount line or paycheck puts it back to review, nothing deleted), and a crash leftover is verified at the top of the next pass. It never moves money and never marks a calendar row paid; the Payroll tab shows Bank: matched / not seen yet / needs your pick per check, and the filing calendar shows Seen in the bank on <date>. Voiding a pay run releases the lines matched to its checks. That release, a changed or deleted calendar row and the owner's Unlink never delete or re-price an expense: the expense the match made stays as it is (the next pass finds it as the existing expense), and one it re-categorized gets its category back. Needs GATE_BANK_IMPORT for the bank lines, GATE_PAYROLL for the pay runs and GATE_PNL_PAY_RUN_WAGES (the match does nothing while that gate is off, so no expense is moved into a category the books do not leave out). Read at call time via payrollBankMatchLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = the bank import, the books and every response are byte for byte today's; rollback = unset (existing links stay).
- `GATE_PAYROLL_COMMISSION_LEDGER` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — basic payroll, owner ruling 2026-10-29, D5 part 3b-2 of the first-technician pay plan: the technician earns 10% of what Waves collects for the NEW service only, in its first 12 months, on work the tech brings in, paid in the next payroll after each payment, even after the tech leaves. Office-booked calls and web plans do not count; collected = net of sales tax, the card surcharge and refunds. Needs GATE_PAYROLL_PAY_LINES and reads estimates.sold_by_technician_id (GATE_COMMISSION_SOLD_BY fills it). On: each pay run (draft, recompute, finalize) records a commission sale for every accepted estimate sold by a W-2 technician, one ledger entry for each customer payment on the sold lines inside the 12 months, and one open 'commission' pay line per payment, so the commission rides on the pay run; a refund inside the 12 months comes off the technician's next commission, never below $0 and never from wages. Read at call time via payrollCommissionLedgerLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = no sales or entries are written, every open commission line comes off the next run, paid lines stay, and with the pay lines gate off every pay run is byte for byte today's; rollback = unset.
- `GATE_PAYROLL_FAMILY_EMPLOYEE` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — basic payroll, owner ruling 2026-10-29, D5 part 2 of the first-technician pay plan: "Build the payroll pieces now, switched off". Needs GATE_PAYROLL. Adds a family-employee setting on a W-2 technician: the relationship (parent or spouse of the owner), the employer entity (sole proprietorship or single-member LLC with no S election) and the date it starts. A pay run dated on or after that date skips federal unemployment tax (26 U.S.C. 3306(c)(5)) and Florida reemployment tax (F.S. 443.1216(13)(d)) for that person and nothing else: income tax withholding, Social Security, Medicare, Form 941 and the W-2 stay the same. The exempt wages are reported as exempt on Form 940 line 4 (box 4e) and left out of the RT-6 wages, employee counts and wage list; they never count toward the $7,000 base. Read at call time via payrollFamilyEmployeeLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = every output and route is byte for byte today's for checks drafted with it off (the setting route answers 404), and a check drafted with it on keeps reporting what it stored; after it is turned off, exempt pay earlier in the year still never uses up the $7,000 base; rollback = unset. Pays, sends and files nothing; the owner makes every payment and filing himself.
- `GATE_PAYROLL_FORM_FIGURES` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — basic payroll layer 3a, owner 2026-10-25, "can we start building basic now, and add layers to it at later stages?". Needs GATE_PAYROLL for the finalized pay runs it reads. Adds a Tax forms section to the Payroll tab and GET /api/admin/payroll/forms: the line-by-line figures to type onto Form 941 and the Florida RT-6 (one quarter), Form 940, and each employee's W-2 with the W-3 totals (one year), added up from finalized, not-voided pay checks by pay date, with a copy button on each amount. Rates come from the payroll calculator. Shows numbers to an admin only: no PDF is filled in, no SSN, EIN or bank number is held (those fields read "Enter from your records"), and nothing is filed, paid or sent. Read at call time via payrollFormFiguresLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = the route answers 404 and the section does not render. Rollback = unset.
- `GATE_PAYROLL_MERCURY_PAY` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — basic payroll option A, owner plan 2026-10-30. Needs GATE_PAYROLL and GATE_PAYROLL_MERCURY_STATUS. Turns on Send to Mercury and Send again on a finalized pay run: for each bank-transfer paycheck with a Mercury recipient id the portal asks Mercury for an APPROVAL REQUEST (POST request-send-money); no money moves until a different Mercury user approves it in the Mercury app, and the portal never calls Mercury's direct send. A request is never sent twice by code: a lost answer becomes an 'unknown' payment the owner resolves. Read at call time via payrollMercuryPayLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = no request is ever made; rollback = unset (existing payment rows stay and still block a void).
- `GATE_PAYROLL_MERCURY_STATUS` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — basic payroll option A, owner plan 2026-10-30: Waves works out each W-2 paycheck and Mercury sends it only after the owner approves it in the Mercury app. Needs GATE_PAYROLL. This is the read-only half: the 15-minute poller that reads each payment's approval request and transaction from Mercury, the recipient check when a Mercury recipient id is saved on a technician, the warnings before a send, and the payment chips on the pay run. Needs the env MERCURY_API_TOKEN (a custom token with read scopes and Send Money with Approval) and MERCURY_ACCOUNT_ID; MERCURY_API_BASE defaults to https://api.mercury.com/api/v1. The token, account number, routing number and any Mercury answer are never logged or stored. Read at call time via payrollMercuryStatusLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = no Mercury call at all and every payroll output and route is byte for byte today's; rollback = unset. Sends nothing to a customer or staff member.
- `GATE_PAYROLL_PAY_LINES` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — basic payroll, owner ruling 2026-10-29, D5 part 1 of the first-technician pay plan: "Build the payroll pieces now, switched off". Needs GATE_PAYROLL (and reads paid time off when GATE_PAYROLL_TIME_OFF is also on). Adds four kinds of pay line to pay runs: a review bonus, a sales commission, a monthly health allowance and an employer HSA contribution. An admin enters a line on the Payroll tab's Pay runs section (payroll_pay_lines) and the next pay run for that technician pays it, even for a technician who has left; a bonus, commission and allowance are taxable wages, while the employer HSA contribution is Section 125 cafeteria-plan money (not wages, not net pay, not in FIT/Social Security/Medicare/FUTA/RT-6 wages). Every line, the HSA one too, counts in the federal regular rate: the extra overtime half-time it causes is paid on the same check, a commission or allowance that covers several weeks is spread equally over those workweeks (29 CFR 778.120) and paid back from each week's approved hours. A covered week with worked time not yet approved makes the technician wait, and one with an unreviewed automatic clock-out holds the check (GATE_SHIFT_CLOSE_REVIEW); once a line is paid, the weeks it priced stay closed to timesheet edits and unlocks until its pay run is voided. W-2 box 12 code TT is filled only for a technician marked FLSA-covered (technicians.flsa_overtime_covered), from qualified_overtime_cents, and code W reports the employer HSA money; Form 940 line 3 includes it and line 4 lists it as exempt fringe benefits. Read at call time via payrollPayLinesLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = every output and route is byte for byte today's for checks drafted with it off (the pay-lines routes answer 404), and a check drafted with it on keeps showing and reporting the figures it stored; rollback = unset. Pays, sends and files nothing; the owner makes every payment and filing himself.
- `GATE_PAYROLL_REVIEW_BONUS_IMPORT` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — basic payroll, owner ruling 2026-10-29, D5 part 3a of the first-technician pay plan: "Build the payroll pieces now, switched off". Needs GATE_PAYROLL and GATE_PAYROLL_PAY_LINES. Each time a pay run is drafted, recomputed or finalized, every earned Google review bonus (review_incentive_payouts) of a W-2 technician, earned on or before the run's week end, becomes an open review bonus pay line (source_type review_incentive_payout) that the run pays; finalizing the run marks the payout paid and voiding it puts the payout back to earned. While GATE_PAYROLL_PAY_LINES is on, an imported line whose payout was marked paid by hand or moved to another technician is dropped at the next draft, recompute or finalize; finalize and void keep the payouts in step with the lines they paid whatever the gates say. Read at call time via payrollReviewBonusImportLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = nothing is imported, every open imported line comes off the next run, and every pay run output is byte for byte today's; rollback = unset. Pays, sends and files nothing; the owner makes every payment himself.
- `GATE_PAYROLL_TAX_CALENDAR` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — basic payroll layer 2, owner 2026-10-25, "can we start building basic now, and add layers to it at later stages?". Needs GATE_PAYROLL for the finalized pay runs it reads. Puts the amount and due date of each payroll tax deposit and filing on the existing Tax page filing calendar: the monthly federal deposit, Form 941, Florida RT-6, a FUTA over $500 alert (FUTA deposits are out of scope), Form 940, W-2 and W-3, and the Florida new hire report. Due dates move past weekends and legal holidays by the IRS and Florida rules. The 14-day reminder text to the owner is the existing tax deadline alert. A row the owner marks filed or paid is never changed; if a pay run later raises an amount above what the marked rows cover, a new balance row holds the difference, and if the pay checks behind a marked return change, a review row asks the owner to check the figures. Read at call time via payrollTaxCalendarLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = the sync returns before any query. The owner makes every deposit and filing himself; sends nothing to a customer or staff member. Rollback = unset.
- `GATE_PAYROLL_TIME_OFF` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — basic payroll, owner ruling 2026-10-26: "Paid days off. The portal's payroll cannot pay time off or holidays. Fix: add this before the tech's first day off." Needs GATE_PAYROLL. Adds paid time off and holidays to pay runs: an admin records a day per W-2 technician (kind pto or holiday, hours up to 24); the pay run pays those hours at the straight hourly rate on the same check, taxed like any wages, and never counts them toward overtime (hours not worked are not hours worked). A week with only time off still makes a check. A per-technician yearly allowance (technicians.pto_hours_per_year, a setting) only warns when paid time off passes it. A record is locked once the pay run for its week is final, whichever technician it paid; void the pay run to change it. The stub, the pay run table, the CSV and the tax package W-2 CSV show the new lines. Read at call time via payrollTimeOffLive(), strict 'true', dark by default; off = every output and route is byte for byte today's. Pays, sends and files nothing; rollback = unset.

Customers OUTSIDE the rail (plan members, payer-billed, invoice-mode, commercial manual billing, one-time) and the setup-only shape keep today's wording. Needs RECURRING_CARD_ON_FILE. The sentence is one row of a closed table in server/services/pet-name-line.js, picked by the re-entry decision the visit's service report already prints (service-report/reentry.js buildReentryContext): a frozen lawn condition (dry, or watered in and dry), or the stored until-dry advisory of a pest, mosquito, lawn, tree and shrub or palm visit with an outside and/or inside re-entry target. Bait or station work, a visit with no re-entry on its report and an advisory not in the table get no sentence. No time or minute figure and no "safe"; the finished sentence still goes through customerCopyViolations. Names come from property_preferences.pets_structured (the customer app's Pets editor): dogs, cats and pets with no type only, one to three names (none or four and more = no sentence), an indoor pet left out of an outside line. It rides as its own paragraph after the template body and before the bundled review line, in every completion-family text except Fast Complete's fixed re-service text (which already carries its own pets line), and only for a performed visit. A failed read costs only the sentence, never the text. Customer copy, so strict opt-in: exactly 'true', read at call time via petNameTextLive(). Pest, lawn and tree & shrub reports only (ruleAnswerReason): termite, rodent, mosquito and specialty reports, typed reports and reports with a visible companion section keep the fixed-rule answer. The recorded instructions the fixed-rule answer states are sent as required lines; a system-written line (watering aftercare, pet precaution, rinse caution) must stand as its own whole sentence of the AI answer word for word, and an answer that carries required lines may hold no dismissal anywhere (ignore, disregard, not true, no longer, outdated, you can skip; before or after the line), else the fixed-rule answer is used; next-visit and schedule questions keep the fixed-rule answer; any technician-sourced required line (recommendations, primary next step, finding recommendations) skips the model and keeps the fixed-rule answer. A miss, a timeout (about 8 s), an empty or rejected answer returns the old fixed-rule answer. The stored event stays question_length and topic; the question text is never stored or logged. Strict opt-in: exactly 'true', read at call time via reportAskAiLive(). Ships DARK; off = the fixed-rule answer alone, byte-identical, no model call.

- `GATE_SPRAY_RAIN_RULES` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — owner 2026-10-26, plan page "Visit-Day Rain Rule", D1 = A and D2 = A: the visit-day spray check judges each product's label rain rule (server/config/label-rain-rules.js: "Do not make applications during rain" on the start hour, a label's dry-before-rain advice (a caution above 60%, the Warning level above 80%), water-in products not judged) and, for a tank spray whose label has no rain rule, the Waves rule for its service (reschedule-rules.js serviceSensitivity). One odds table for every rain number: caution above reschedule-rules caution_if_rain_prob_above (60%). Owner 2026-10-29 ("i just want to warn techs"): at `true` no weather reading withholds a dose anywhere (tech card, tank search, Tree & Shrub guide); a reading past reschedule_if_rain_prob_above (80%) or a wind or temperature limit is a caution that names it, the guide shows its mix when only the forecast is missing (an open label review still withholds it), and the Dispatch chip names the caution and the product ("Rain warning · Celsius WG", "Wind caution · Celsius WG") ahead of "Weather unknown" and is never red. Step 2: rain past 80% is the Warning level; wind and temperature are judged at the start hour and the next one; a tank spray whose label gives no dry time gets the Waves hours; a label heat line (Celsius: no adjuvant above 90°F) is a caution; each caution carries one action. Owner 2026-10-30: a label wind line is a caution at each label's own number (Cyper TC's on-grade line on termite visits only), and a fertilizer's "Do not apply if heavy rain is expected" is a caution when the start hour's forecast words say heavy rain or thunderstorms, on the same odds tiers. `shadow` = the live check is unchanged and the new verdict of each visit-day readiness / card build is upserted to spray_rain_rule_shadow (peak per visit per day); `true` keeps upserting the same row, which then counts the real rain odds. Off = byte-identical to today. Canonical CALL-TIME reader sprayRainRulesMode(). Kill switch: unset.

Strict opt-in, read at call time via visitPrepPestReadLive(); ALSO requires GATE_VISIT_PREP_PHOTOS and GATE_VISIT_FACTS live (the tech facts block is the only place a read is shown). Triggered from services/visit-prep.js's createVisitPrepSubmission — the ONE place a submission is created — fire-and-forget AFTER the submission's own transaction commits, never on the request path. A submission whose visit is a pest-only service (pest-production-calibration.js isPestOnlyServiceType — never a WDO inspection or assessment; a grouped stop counts if ANY live member at the same physical stop is), runs the same photo-id-v2 identifyPestV2 the app's Photo ID route calls and stores the result in pest_identifications with source='visit_prep', mode='internal' (already an allowed mode — no migration on that table). Lawn / tree & shrub / anything else resolves straight to read_status='unsupported', no engine call. VISIT_PREP_READ_DAILY_CAP (default 40) is this feature's OWN cap, separate from Photo ID's; a capped or failed read never blocks the submission — the technician still gets the photos, just with read_status='failed'. See docs in services/visit-prep-pest-read.js.
- `GATE_VISIT_PREP_PHOTOS` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — server-only dark foundation: customer attaches photos + a short note to a specific upcoming visit from the public /appointment/:token page — POST /api/public/appointment/:token/photos, plus an additive prepPhotos summary on the existing GET. Strict opt-in, read at call time via visitPrepPhotosLive(). The appointment-page entry ALSO needs GATE_APPOINTMENT_PAGE (it rides that router). The Waves app entry — POST /api/schedule/:id/prep-photos and the prepPhotos field on GET /api/schedule/next — needs only this gate; flipping it opens both entry points. Off = the generic 404 on both POST routes: the anonymous appointment-page route answers it before any body parse (visitPrepPreParserGuard, mounted ahead of the shared parsers in index.js); the authenticated app route answers it after the schedule router's normal authentication and shared parsers (a logged-in route exposes nothing to an anonymous probe), before its own limiter and multipart parse. Neither GET payload carries a prepPhotos key. Surfaces when on: the appointment-page box, the app's Send photos button, the tech Visit Brief's Customer flagged block, the stop chip, and the office feed item. Sends nothing to a customer.
- `GATE_VISIT_PREP_PLANT_READ` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — sibling of GATE_VISIT_PREP_PEST_READ — automatic lawn / tree & shrub read of a visit-prep submission's photos, dark. Strict opt-in, read at call time via visitPrepPlantReadLive(); ALSO requires GATE_VISIT_PREP_PHOTOS and GATE_VISIT_FACTS live, same as the pest read. Triggered from the SAME single place, services/visit-prep.js's createVisitPrepSubmission, as its own independent fire-and-forget call — never disturbs the pest-read or tech-alert hooks there. A submission whose stop is a strict lawn-only or tree & shrub-only service (visit-prep-plant-applicability.js isLawnOnlyServiceType/isTreeShrubOnlyServiceType — never WDO, termite, or a Waves Assessment) runs the merged photo-id-v2 lawn/plant engine (identifyPlantV2, photo-id-v2/plant-engine.js — L3, no customer route yet) and stores the workup on the submission's own `read_result` jsonb column (no second table; the pest read's `read_ref` is untouched). A combined Lawn & Pest stop (one combined service_type such as "Lawn Care + Pest Control", or separate pest and lawn / tree & shrub members) gets BOTH reads under ONE claim (services/visit-prep-combo-read.js, read key 'combo:<subject>') only while BOTH this gate and GATE_VISIT_PREP_PEST_READ are live (owner ruling 2026-09-30); with one dark the one live engine reads that stop alone; the combo read counts 2 against the daily cap (two vision calls). Anything else resolves straight to read_status='unsupported', no engine call. Shares the pest read's SAME VISIT_PREP_READ_DAILY_CAP (default 40) and the same advisory lock key — deliberately ONE cap across both engines, not a second one; a capped or failed read never blocks the submission. See docs in services/visit-prep-plant-read.js.
- `GATE_VISIT_PREP_READ_SWEEP` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — visit-prep read recovery sweep, dark. Strict opt-in via visitPrepReadSweepLive(); ALSO requires a live read engine (visitPrepPestReadLive() or visitPrepPlantReadLive()). Every 15 minutes (scheduler.js, checked BEFORE the cron lock, so off = no query, no write) it re-runs the same dispatchVisitPrepRead (services/visit-prep-read-dispatch.js, which runs only live engines) for at most 10 submissions from the last 14 ET days on still-upcoming, join-eligible visits: read_status 'none' older than 15 minutes (never attempted: photo load failed, claim error, stop moved), 'unsupported' where a live engine now reads the stop, and a 'done' or 'failed' read made by the wrong engine or subject for the stop as it is now (released to 'none', then re-read; a row checked and needing nothing is skipped for an hour). Pending rows, and failed rows on the line they failed on, are never retried. One retry per row per case, per settled attempt for a stale read (activity_log markers); each retry claims only from the status it selected and counts against VISIT_PREP_READ_DAILY_CAP on the ET day it runs, like a first read. A batch with failed retries fails the job run (job_health).
- `GATE_VISIT_PREP_TECH_ALERTS` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — PR 6, customer-visit-photos scope doc §5.4 item 4: the assigned field technician gets a tech-home card plus a one-line push — "A customer sent photos for a visit on your route" — when a customer submits visit prep photos. Strict opt-in, read at call time via visitPrepTechAlertsLive() in server/services/visit-prep-tech-alert.js. ALSO requires GATE_VISIT_PREP_PHOTOS live (nothing to alert about otherwise). Deliberately its OWN gate rather than GATE_TECH_VISIT_NOTIFICATIONS — that gate is off in production, and this can be the first tech visit card to go live; it reuses that mechanism's card/push plumbing (tech_notifications + PushService.sendToAdminUser) without depending on its gate. Recipient = the visit's CURRENT assigned technician, re-read fresh at send time — never the pre-lock row. No technician, or a non-assignable one → no card. Never fired for a duplicate-only resubmit. Sends nothing to a customer.

- `GATE_WARRANTY_13692_CHECK` — `server/config/feature-gates.js` — owner 2026-10-24, Rule 5E-14.105(1): the New Construction Termite Warranty and the Builder Master Agreement read the signed FDACS-13692 consumer notice on file (an e-signed Document Request or an office-uploaded scan of the paper form) instead of a typed date. On or before the notice grace end (2026-11-24) a missing notice only warns and the typed date still works; after it the issue refuses until a notice dated on or before the contract basis date and the treatment date is on file. Adds the admin consumer-notices routes. Read at call time via warranty13692CheckLive(), strict 1/true/on, dark by default; off = typed date only, routes answer 404. Sends nothing to a customer.
